Any scalar detector reduces to its efficacy, ef = |E_1 f - E_0 f| / sqrt(Var_0(f)) <= rho, which can be estimated from controls before the audit is run. A separate sample-split certificate lower-bounds alpha distribution-free, without requiring an orientation assumption. Our empirical finding is two-sided. Frozen calibration efficacy predicts held-out power curves, with R^2 = 0.83-0.98 across six exact-permutation channels, but the efficacy-only Gaussian budget is miscalibrated at the small sample sizes it prescribes, failing in 9/9 gate-passing channels even though efficacy itself transports. The failure is in the inversion, not the calibration. A predeclared two-stage planner that simulates the deployed test repairs the budgets, is uniformly conservative, and abstains when its probe does not transport. The certificate is valid but vacuous at audit scale, and a five-seed paired injection study recovers the mechanism ordering verbatim > paraphrase > surface, in which the apparent answer-only signal is explained by baseline drift. We report the audit contract and its failures together: a non-rejection is interpretable only alongside the efficacy, budget, and validity gates that produced it.

</details>


### [145] [TongGuOCR: A Layout-Aware and Token-Augmented OCR Framework for Chinese Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07917)
*Zhongheng Zhou,Yi Sun,Huiguo He,Yuyi Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Yulin Fang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向中文历史文献的布局感知与令牌增强型OCR框架TongGuOCR，有效提升了复杂版式和生僻字场景下的识别精度，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文历史文献常具有复杂版式、生僻字及非线性阅读顺序，传统OCR方法难以准确识别，限制了数字化与计算分析。因此需要一种能处理这些挑战的新框架。

Method: 提出TongGuOCR，包含两个核心模块：1）布局感知预处理模块，构建并优化局部连贯的识别块以保留上下文；2）令牌增强识别模块，在字符级扩展词表以直接编码生僻字，并引入离散的空间位移令牌建模行间跳转关系，引导解码器处理复杂阅读路径。

Result: 在两个中文历史文档OCR基准上实验表明，TongGuOCR优于传统的专用OCR模型、通用多模态大模型（MLLMs）及OCR专用MLLM。在更具挑战性的M5HisDoc基准上，达到93.76 AR，NED从10.43降至6.15，RO-ED从7.53降至3.49。

Conclusion: TongGuOCR通过布局感知与双层级令牌增强机制，显著提升了中文历史文献的OCR性能，为古籍数字化提供了高效可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Chinese historical documents preserve valuable cultural heritage, but many collections remain accessible only as scanned page images, preventing full-text retrieval, collation, and computational analysis. Optical character recognition (OCR) can bridge this gap, but accurate transcription remains challenging because historical documents often contain complex layouts, rare characters, and nontrivial reading orders. We propose TongGuOCR, a layout-aware and token-augmented OCR framework for Chinese historical documents. First, a Layout-Aware Preprocessing module constructs and refines locally coherent recognition blocks to preserve local context while reducing interference across regions. Second, a Token-Augmented Recognition module augments the transcription target at two complementary levels: character-level vocabulary expansion gives each rare glyph a direct one-token representation and shortens its decoding path, while line-to-line transition modeling injects discrete spatial displacement tokens that guide the decoder along complex reading paths without requiring precise coordinates. Experiments on two Chinese historical document OCR benchmarks show that TongGuOCR outperforms representative traditional task-specific OCR models, general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs), and OCR-oriented MLLMs. On the more challenging M5HisDoc benchmark, TongGuOCR achieves 93.76 AR and reduces NED from 10.43 to 6.15 and RO-ED from 7.53 to 3.49 relative to the best competing score for each metric. An online demo is available at https://jzzh2004.github.io/TongGuOCR.

</details>


### [146] [ZhuLong: Execution-Grounded LLM Agent for EDA Scripting with Offline API Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07925)
*Yang Liu,Shiwei Hou,Xiyuan Chen,Yu Wang,Sen Yuan,Qirui Gan,Shao You,Feifan Chen,Wencheng Li,Shuyang Hu,Yongzhou Liu,Emma Xia,Xiaojing Lu,Hao Wang,Fan Xu,Yanfeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ZhuLong，一个针对EDA脚本编写的基于执行的LLM代理，通过API检索、文档检查、沙箱执行和自探索机制，在真实环境中显著提升了任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型难以应对电子设计自动化（EDA）中工具特定且常未文档化的API编程问题，导致脚本编写效率低下。

Method: 结合API检索、文档检查、统一MCP工具支持的沙箱执行，并引入离线API自探索机制，通过反事实实验推断未记录的API行为。

Result: 在包含158个真实任务的EDA-Eval-PyAether基准上，ZhuLong在商业Empyrean Aether环境中达到78.5%的Pass@1；消融实验显示沙箱执行是主要贡献因素，自探索机制减少了22.1%的工具调用并提升3.2个百分点准确率；在20个交互式任务中，PyAether和SKILL分别达到60.0%和50.0%的Pass@1。

Conclusion: ZhuLong通过执行反馈与自探索机制有效解决了EDA领域脚本生成中的未文档化API难题，显著优于纯LLM基线方法。

Abstract: EDA scripting with tool-specific, often undocumented APIs remains a long-tail bottleneck that existing LLMs fail to address. This paper presents ZhuLong, an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that combines API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, augmented by an offline API self-exploration mechanism that infers undocumented API behaviors through counterfactual experimentation.
  We evaluate ZhuLong on EDA-Eval-PyAether, a benchmark of 158 real-world tasks with assertion-based execution, where the complete system achieves 78.5% Pass@1 in the commercial Empyrean Aether environment, substantially outperforming a pure LLM baseline (23.6%). Ablation studies identify sandbox execution as the dominant performance driver (41.2 pp drop when removed), with the self-exploration mechanism contributing an additional 3.2 pp accuracy gain and a 22.1% reduction in per-task tool calls. On 20 interactive tasks involving unsaved layouts and schematics, ZhuLong achieves 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

</details>


### [147] [REIN: Bridging the Gap between Reasoning and Reliability via Reflection and Abstention Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07931)
*Zhengze Huang,Luyang Yu,Di Hong,Xinzhe Huang,Wanyu Lin,Zhixuan Chu,Zhan Qin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REIN是一种对齐框架，通过结构化推理和显式自我反思减少大推理模型中的幻觉，提升选择性准确率并维持高覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型容易产生幻觉，源于推理错误和知识缺失，影响可靠性和安全性，需有效方法加以抑制。

Method: 提出REIN框架，训练模型生成包含反思的结构化推理链，并引入奖励机制鼓励在无正确推理路径时主动 abstain，从而减少幻觉。

Result: 在数学与常识推理基准上，REIN显著降低58~72%的幻觉代理指标，保持86~91%的覆盖率，选择性准确率提升6.6~14.2%。

Conclusion: REIN能有效缓解大推理模型的两类幻觉，在单次前向传递中实现更安全、可靠的推理，无需额外监督或复杂解码过程。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are prone to hallucination, which undermines their reliability and poses challenges for safe deployment. Hallucinations in LRMs arise from two distinct failure sources: reasoning hallucination, where flawed inference steps propagate to an incorrect conclusion, and knowledge hallucination, where the model lacks the requisite factual knowledge to answer the query. To address reasoning hallucination, we propose REIN, an alignment framework that trains LRMs to produce a structured reasoning sequence, $\texttt{<think>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<reflection>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<answer>}$, enabling explicit self-reflection before committing to a final answer. To address knowledge hallucination, REIN introduces a reward mechanism that encourages explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when none of the sampled reasoning chains yields a correct answer, allowing the model to refrain from unsupported predictions. Extensive evaluations on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that REIN consistently improves selective accuracy, reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses, and maintains high coverage compared with competitive baselines. Notably, REIN achieves these gains within a single forward pass, without requiring process supervision, inference-time controllers, external search, or multi-round critiques. Experiments on multiple backbones show that REIN reduces the hallucination proxy by $58\sim72\%$ relative to the base models while maintaining $86\sim91\%$ average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by $6.6\sim14.2\%$.

</details>


### [148] [Locating Failure in Multi-Page Visually Rich Document Understanding: An Empirical Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07943)
*Lewei Xu,Yihao Ding,Zihan Xu,Daniel Yitian Su,Daochang Liu,Siwen Luo,Yifan Peng,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多页视觉丰富文档理解（MP-VRDU）中的三大失败模式：表示、选择和推理，并通过实验分析各因素对系统性能的影响，提出在计算资源有限的情况下构建此类系统的实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MP-VRDU系统存在相互矛盾且缺乏验证的设计主张，作者旨在识别导致错误的根本原因，并系统性地评估不同组件的贡献与局限。

Method: 作者在一个多页文档理解数据集上，通过控制变量的方式分别干预表示、选择和推理三个模块，隔离并分析每个失败模式的影响；同时测试视觉输入、文本提取、页面完整性、干扰项和提示策略的作用。

Result: 研究发现：视觉信息必要但不能替代文本提取；缺失关键页面严重限制准确率，而干扰页面影响较小；即使证据完整提供，推理模型仍难以跨页整合信息；提示工程可显著改变推理行为，改善部分表现但可能损害其他方面。

Conclusion: 构建高效的MP-VRDU系统应在固定计算预算下优先保障文本提取质量与关键页面的覆盖，并谨慎设计提示以平衡推理行为，未来需增强模型跨页推理能力。

Abstract: Multi-page visually-rich document understanding (MP-VRDU) requires managing evidence that is sparse, spread across pages, and often exceeds a model's context window. Prior work has produced competing, largely untested claims about how these systems should be built. We attribute incorrect answers to three failure modes, representation, selection, and reasoning, and isolate each over a multi-page document understanding dataset by intervening on one while holding the others fixed. We find that vision is necessary but does not replace text extraction, that missing pages bound accuracy while distractors cost little, and that reasoners fail to integrate evidence across pages even when it is fully supplied. Prompting can shift reasoning behaviour substantially, improving some outcomes at the expense of others. We translate these findings into guidance for building such systems under a fixed compute budget.

</details>


### [149] [Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution for Verification of Distributed Parallel AI Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07947)
*Gautham Koorma,Vikas Sharma,George Edwards,Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DNSSE的混合测试框架，结合了大语言模型引导的调度预测、符号约束求解和覆盖率引导的随机变异，用于检测分布式并行AI程序中的并发错误。该方法在真实基准上显著提高了分支覆盖率并发现了更多缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试方法无法有效应对分布式并行AI程序中的非确定性执行、高维输入和非线性操作带来的可靠性问题，因此需要一种新的测试方法来弥补这一差距。

Method: 将分布式AI执行建模为非确定性转换系统，使用线性时序逻辑指定正确性，并结合大语言模型（LLM）引导的调度预测、符号求解和基于覆盖率的随机变异，构建了DNSSE混合测试框架。同时证明了混合求解器的正确性和完备性，并分析了LLM引导调度探索的期望成本。

Result: 在PyTorch和Ray上实现了可扩展的DNSSE系统，在五个真实的分布式AI基准上，比最强基线多检测出2.9%的并发错误，平均分支覆盖率从68.6%提升至91.6%。

Conclusion: DNSSE通过融合神经符号方法与随机执行，有效提升了分布式AI程序的测试覆盖率和缺陷检测能力，为解决AI系统中复杂的并发可靠性问题提供了新路径。

Abstract: Distributed parallel Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs expose reliability gaps that conventional testing cannot close: parallel executions are non-deterministic, and AI workloads bring high-dimensional inputs and non-linear operations that defeat fuzzing and symbolic execution in isolation. We present Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution (DNSSE), a hybrid testing framework that couples schedule prediction guided by a Large Language Model (LLM) with symbolic constraint solving and coverage-guided stochastic mutation. We model distributed AI executions as non-deterministic transition systems, specify correctness in linear temporal logic, and prove soundness, bounded completeness, and probabilistic completeness of the hybrid solver, together with an expected-cost analysis of LLM-guided schedule exploration. A scalable implementation on PyTorch and Ray detects 2.9% more concurrency bugs than the strongest baseline and raises average branch coverage from 68.6 % to 91.6 % across five realistic distributed AI benchmarks.

</details>


### [150] [Guixu: Valuation-Driven Data Discovery for Autonomous AI Agents with On-Chain Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07949)
*Yifan Wu,Yuchen Peng,Jiaqi Chai,Yufei Qian,Xilin Li,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自主智能体的估值驱动数据发现系统Guixu，通过任务感知的数据估价、预算约束下的采购协议和链上验证机制，实现更智能、可信的数据发现与获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据发现系统多为检索导向，缺乏对特定任务效用评估、预算约束下数据选择及可信使用反馈的支持，难以满足自主智能体对高效、可靠数据获取的需求。

Method: Guixu采用三阶段估值流水线，结合代理标签传播和多轮背包优化进行任务感知的数据估值；引入智能体支付协议支持预算约束的数据采购，并利用链上数据市场与证明信号实现可验证的数据发现。

Result: 系统能够有效支持从自然语言任务描述到多源搜索、数据估值及可验证交易反馈的全流程，显著提升数据发现的任务相关性、经济性和可信度。

Conclusion: Guixu推动了数据发现从关键词检索向任务感知、预算感知和可信化转变，为自主智能体提供了更高效的外部数据利用方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly rely on external data to complete downstream tasks such as model training and decision support. However, existing data discovery systems remain largely retrieval-oriented: they surface candidate datasets from heterogeneous sources, but provide limited support for estimating task-specific utility, selecting cost-effective datasets under budget constraints, or incorporating trustworthy feedback from prior usage. This paper presents Guixu, a valuation-driven data discovery system for autonomous agents. Guixu employs a three-phase valuation pipeline with proxy-label propagation and multi-round knapsack optimization for task-aware data valuation. Guixu integrates agentic payment protocol to enable budget-constrained data procurement workflows. Guixu leverages on-chain data market and attestation signals for verifiable data discovery. Our demonstration highlights how Guixu enables an agent to move beyond keyword-based dataset retrieval toward task- and budget-aware, trustworthy data discovery and procurement. Attendees can interactively explore the full workflow, from NL task specification and multi-source search to data valuation and verifiable transaction feedback.

</details>


### [151] [KGCache: Amortized Subgraph Retrieval for KG Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07954)
*Uros Stanic,Changcheng Yuan,Sabuj Laskar,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了KGCache，一种用于知识图谱问答（KGQA）中一跳知识图谱邻居的内存缓存系统，能够兼容ToG和RoG两种范式，通过缓存重复访问的实体显著提升查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于知识图谱的大语言模型问答中，不同问题常反复查询相同的图邻域，造成冗余检索，影响效率。

Method: 设计并实现KGCache，部署于KGQA引擎与后端知识图谱之间，支持LRU、LFU及Oracle策略的实体级缓存，并探索基于相似查询的语义缓存以进一步提升命中率。

Result: 实验表明WebQSP和CWQ数据集中存在大量实体重用；KGCache最高可将知识图谱检索加速1.91倍，语义上下文缓存最高带来1.06倍的端到端系统加速，单次命中最快可达3.73倍提速。

Conclusion: KGCache有效减少了KGQA中的重复查询开销，提升了系统效率，具备对多种KGQA范式的兼容性和良好的加速潜力。

Abstract: Large language models can answer knowledge-intensive questions more reliably when they are grounded with knowledge graphs, but systems such as Think-on-Graph and Reasoning-on-Graph repeatedly query the same graph neighborhoods across different questions. In this work, we study this repeated retrieval in Knowledge Graph Question Answering~(KGQA) workloads and propose KGCache, an in-memory cache for one-hop knowledge graph neighborhoods. KGCache is designed to be compatible with both iterative traversal (ToG) and one shot planning (RoG) KGQA paradigms. KGCache is placed between the KGQA engine and the backend serving the KG, so repeated entity requests can be served from cache instead of issuing new KG queries. We evaluate KGCache on WebQSP and CWQ using LRU, LFU, and a trace-aware Oracle policy. Our analysis shows that both datasets contain substantial entity reuse among starting entities and entities reached during traversal. We also explore semantic caching for similar queries, which shows additional hit-rate gains on WebQSP and needs further accuracy testing on CWQ. Entity caching accelerates KG retrieval by up to $1.91\times$, while semantic-context caching achieves up to $1.06\times$ full-system speedup in the evaluated WebQSP configurations, with each hit being up to $3.73\times$ faster.

</details>


### [152] [Self-Evolving Neuro-Symbolic Skills for Tool-Augmented Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07955)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuo-Xia Wang,Xuan-Yi Zhu,Zhi Zhou,Xinwei Yang,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Yang Chen,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSy-Spatial是一种神经符号框架，旨在通过自我演化空间技能来提升大视觉语言模型在细粒度空间任务中的推理准确性和工具使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型在需要精确空间感知和几何计算的细粒度空间任务上表现不可靠，而现有工具增强方法存在缺乏依赖约束或泛化能力差的问题。

Method: 提出NeSy-Spatial框架，将工具交互和几何操作抽象为可执行的原子指令，并构建两类互补技能：用于组织工具执行的工具使用技能和用于结构化几何推理的几何技能；通过检索和执行相关技能进行闭环推理，并在进化过程中分析成功与失败轨迹以优化技能结构。

Result: 在三个空间推理基准上的实验表明，NeSy-Spatial能够随着更精确的工具利用持续提高推理准确性。

Conclusion: NeSy-Spatial通过自演化机制有效提升了多模态模型在复杂空间任务中的性能，展示了神经符号方法在增强空间推理方面的潜力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, but they remain unreliable on fine-grained spatial tasks that demand both precise spatial perception and fine-grained geometric computation beyond end-to-end generation. Tool augmentation offers a natural solution, while existing methods either plan tool calls from scratch without explicit dependency constraints or rely on fixed pipelines that are redundant and generalize poorly across spatial tasks. An effective spatial reasoning agent should instead accumulate reusable experience and adaptively compose it for new problems. To this end, we propose NeSy-Spatial, a neuro-symbolic framework for self-evolving spatial skills. NeSy-Spatial abstracts tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions and composes them into two complementary skill types: Tool-Use Skills for organizing tool execution and Geometry Skills for structured geometric reasoning. During inference, NeSy-Spatial retrieves and executes relevant skills in a closed-loop process. During evolution, it analyzes buffered successful and failed trajectories to refine skill structures and prune unreliable or inactive entries. Experiments on three spatial reasoning benchmarks show that NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy with more precise tool utilization.

</details>


### [153] [SCOUT: Self-Checking and Recovery-Aware Tool-Thought Agents for Ultra-Long Egocentric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07959)
*Keyang Zhong,Kuo Wang,Peng Liu,Quanlong Zheng,Junlin Xie,Zhijia Liang,Yanhao Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT框架和UPS-GRPO算法，用于提升超长第一人称视频理解中的多跳推理能力，通过自适应策略和改进的强化学习方法实现更优的长时程信用分配与探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理超长视频时受限于上下文长度和关键片段定位能力，且当前的CoTT系统因缺乏恢复机制而容易产生错误传播，同时强化学习训练中稀疏奖励导致长时推理的信用分配不佳。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，引入可动态权衡利用与探索的自适应策略；设计UPS-GRPO方法，基于不确定性优先的策略优化，并结合回合级优势分解，融合结果奖励与工具对齐的时间奖励。

Result: 实验表明SCOUT在超长第一人称视频基准上达到最先进性能，同时在较短的长视频任务中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SCOUT与UPS-GRPO有效提升了超长视频理解中多步工具使用的鲁棒性与学习效率，为长时程多模态推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Ultra-long egocentric video understanding requires reasoning over temporally sparse evidence distributed across hours or days, challenging current multimodal models with limited context and the grounding of key video segments. While Chain-of-Tool-Thought (CoTT) agent systems enable iterative retrieval and inspection, they suffer from error propagation due to rigid zoom-in strategies that lack recovery mechanisms. In this work, we address these challenges through SCOUT (Self-Checking Chain-Of-Tool-thought), a recovery-aware agentic framework introducing an adaptive policy that evaluates intermediate tool observations and dynamically trades off exploitation (zoom-in) and exploration (region switching), enabling robust multi-hop reasoning over extremely long horizons. However, training such multi-turn tool-using agents remains challenging, as existing RL methods rely on sparse outcome-level rewards and lack supervision over extended decision trajectories, resulting in suboptimal credit assignment for long-horizon reasoning. To address this, we develop UPS-GRPO, an uncertainty-prioritized policy optimization method that concentrates exploration on high-uncertainty post-tool states while preserving sample efficiency. We further introduce a turn-level advantage decomposition that integrates outcome rewards with tool-grounded temporal alignment rewards for improved credit assignment. Experiments show that SCOUT achieves state-of-the-art results on ultra-long egocentric benchmarks, while remaining competitive on shorter-horizon long-video settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行器级策略的深度强化学习方法，用于控制缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPR），该方法不依赖于特定机器人构型，具有良好的泛化能力，并在仿真和真实平台上验证了其鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL方法训练时间长、难以泛化到不同CDPR构型，且通常依赖复杂的正向运动学建模，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出执行器级策略（ALP），每个电机独立学习达到目标缆绳长度的控制策略；通过共享策略实现对任意CDPR构型的通用控制，并利用逆运动学简化问题求解；在仿真中训练后迁移到真实机器人。

Result: 所提方法在仿真和真实8电机3D CDPR上成功验证，策略可跨构型迁移（从4电机2D仿真迁移到8电机3D实体）；相比传统DRL方法具有更高鲁棒性和控制精度。

Conclusion: 执行器级DRL策略能够有效提升CDPR控制的通用性与实用性，为多构型缆索驱动机器人的快速部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [155] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SC²-WM的自校正世界模型框架，通过引入内部反馈实现闭环决策，提升了视觉-语言导航在连续环境中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖开环执行，缺乏在推理过程中检测和纠正内部状态漂移的机制，导致导航性能受限。

Method: 提出SC²-WM框架，利用世界模型的前瞻性生成反馈，进行状态级计划优化；并引入条件式世界感知适应机制，在测试时根据反馈选择性更新世界模型以应对挑战场景。

Result: 在标准VLN-CE基准上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了导航的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SC²-WM通过闭环反馈和动态模型调整，有效解决了部分可观环境下的状态漂移问题，为视觉-语言导航提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [156] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督的视觉操作方法，机器人通过腕部RGB图像自动生演示并学习相对位姿校正，无需外部标定，实现了从粗略接近到精细对齐的抓取控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法依赖特定对象编程、人工标注或标定感知流程，限制了实际场景中的快速部署；而示教学习虽更直接，但仍需人工参与。因此需要一种减少人为干预和标定负担的自监督学习方法。

Method: 利用ROS 2和Isaac Sim自动采集带标签的图像-位姿对，分别构建平面精调和三维粗略接近的数据集，训练卷积网络从单帧RGB图像回归相对平移与旋转，并采用由粗到精的控制器实现抓取。

Result: 在仿真中，精调阶段将最终平面分散度从9.69 mm降至5.38 mm；在真实UR5e机器人上的实验显示，对三个物体的端到端抓取成功率分别为66.6%和63.6%，并在旋转条件下保持一定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自动生成的演示可用于支持低配置成本下的实用化视觉操作，但深度预测和物体泛化仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [157] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AgenticRobotics的后端无关控制框架，利用LLM控制器驱动可丢弃的工作进程，通过持久的训练-评估-改进事务实现机器人策略的自主优化，强调基于证据的质量管控与可恢复状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于机器人领域中的工具（如训练策略、数据收集等）经常失败，需要一种能够持续测量、记录工具质量并在底层构件变更时使其失效的架构，以实现可靠的自动化策略改进。

Method: 设计了一个由LLM控制器主导的代理系统架构，包含不可变目标、控制器掌控的度量机制、基于提交密钥的崩溃恢复、证据分级的技能库以及标准化调用接口的工具注册表，支持故障注入下的零重复或丢失操作。

Result: 系统实现了基于证据的晋升机制，显著降低了错误晋升率（从0.005–0.021降至0.001），支持在任意时刻有效决策，并能捕捉六类构件篡改行为，验证了系统的鲁棒性与安全性。

Conclusion: AgenticRobotics通过记录驱动和证据把关，使操作员可以安全退出循环，实现了可恢复、防误晋升、抗干扰的机器人策略自动化改进，其优势在于过程可靠性而非单纯选择更优检查点。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [158] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroDPO，一种无需人工标注的自动化直接偏好优化方法，通过物理仿真回滚生成高质量训练数据，使轻量级2B模型在无人机视觉语言导航中实现更强鲁棒性和更低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法依赖大语言模型，难以部署于边缘设备；同时纯行为克隆缺乏负反馈，导致在分布外场景下鲁棒性差、碰撞率高。

Method: 提出AeroDPO框架：利用确定性物理仿真进行状态回滚，自动提取因果推理错误作为拒绝样本，结合解耦的特权干预生成避障偏好动作，并使用离线视觉语言检查器过滤视觉模糊样本，构建零成本自动化数据闭环。

Result: 轻量级2B模型在未见环境中成功率达到49.16%，显著降低碰撞率，超越现有基线，成为无人机自主导航新SOTA。

Conclusion: 感知质量比语言模型规模更重要，结合自动化偏好学习可有效提升小模型在复杂3D环境中的导航性能与安全性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [159] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对触觉/力感知机器人学习的统一分类框架TF-ART，整合多模态感知与多阶段系统设计，填补了现有综述在该领域的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述缺乏从统一视角对力和触觉感知机器人学习中多模态感知与多阶段架构的系统性梳理。

Method: 提出TF-ART分类法，将现有方法纳入统一的层次结构，分析其在感知模态组织、异构传感融合、多阶段动作生成与低层控制连接等方面的设计。

Result: 建立了涵盖算法设计与实际部署需求的全面框架，厘清了当前研究的技术路径与系统架构。

Conclusion: TF-ART为力和触觉感知机器人学习提供了系统性的分类与分析工具，推动物理交互智能的进一步发展。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [160] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的模型预测路径积分控制方法（PR-MPPI），通过在动力学采样过程中引入投影-回缩机制，精确满足操作任务中的等式与不等式约束，显著提升了在多自由度双臂系统中对闭链运动、关节限位和避障等复杂约束的处理能力，并在仿真与真实人形机器人平台上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MPPI控制仅通过代价函数以软惩罚方式处理约束，难以保证关键约束（如闭链运动、关节极限）在整个运动过程中的严格满足，尤其在强任务成本下容易失效，因此需要一种能精确强制执行约束的新方法。

Method: 提出Projection-Retraction MPPI（PR-MPPI）：在每次 rollout 的每一步中，将采样的速度先投影到满足等式约束的子空间，再在此子空间内将不等式约束投影到对应的半空间；随后通过回缩步骤将最终命令拉回到约束流形上，确保等式约束满足至数值精度。

Result: 在14自由度双臂系统上验证：仿真实验表明该方法在整个运动过程中将闭链等式约束保持在数值精度范围内，并成功通过关节限位压力测试和随机避障测试；在Unitree H1-2人形机器人上的实物实验实现了双臂对移动障碍物的反应式避障。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI能够在非凸优化背景下精确满足操作任务中的多重约束，克服了传统MPPI在硬约束处理上的不足，为高维机器人系统的安全、可靠控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [161] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种将几何智能嵌入到结构化材料系统中的新型机器人建造方法，通过自对准复合嵌套晶格模块实现可逆连接、三维聚合与结构性能的统一，并验证了其在家具和建筑尺度上的可重构应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造系统将材料与机器人分开设计，智能主要集中于硬件与控制；本研究旨在通过材料本身的几何设计集成多功能，减少对外部感知与精密控制的依赖。

Method: 设计了一种由共连的截角八面体-八面体单元组成的自对准复合嵌套晶格模块，利用截角八面体部分实现机器人抓取与对准，八面体部分集成可拆卸卡扣连接器，并采用嵌套排列实现三轴互锁聚合。

Result: 实现了由机械臂和移动组装器搭建的家具与建筑尺度结构，包括座椅、跨度结构、表面与竖向框架；压缩测试显示模块刚度为4,556 N/mm，最大载荷3,445 N，抗压模量17.5 MPa；模块可拆卸并重复用于不同构型。

Conclusion: 通过将几何智能内置于材料系统，可实现高效、可重构、支持循环使用的 robotic construction，推动向去中心化智能建造的发展。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [162] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LIRA的局部跨层动作调节机制，通过深度感知的信息路由，提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作任务中的性能，尤其在零样本迁移和分布偏移下表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型仅利用预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的部分表示层次，或僵化地将解码器与特定VLM层对应，限制了跨层级任务信息的融合，因此需要更灵活的特征路由机制。

Method: LIRA基于任务标记特征和从VLM中间状态提取的LIRA查询特征，为每个并行融合块分配一个以对应VLM层为中心的局部窗口，并聚合邻近层的查询特征，结合任务特征和本体感知输入进行动作预测。该方法不改变主干网络、解码器结构或训练方式。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实世界操作任务上，LIRA在相同0.5B参数配置下优于VLA-Adapter基线；在LIBERO-Plus的零样本迁移中，平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提高了18.9个百分点。

Conclusion: LIRA通过深度感知的局部跨层信息路由，有效增强了VLA模型对多层级语义信息的利用能力，显著提升了模型在复杂操作任务中的表现与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [163] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，通过结合物体几何和触觉反馈来实现接触感知的抓取生成与触觉引导的自适应优化，显著提升了机器人抓取的成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致鲁棒性和泛化能力受限。

Method: 设计了一种高效的视觉-触觉表征方法，将触觉信号与手指身份关联，紧密融合物体几何与触觉反馈，支持规划阶段的接触感知抓取生成和接触后的触觉引导优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中实验表明，该方法显著提高了不同物体上的抓取成功率和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的视觉-触觉融合框架有效实现了稳定且自适应的机器人抓取，为未来灵巧操作提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [164] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于扩展现实（XR）的平台，实现6自由度导管实时跟踪与患者特异性3D心脏模型内的可视化，显著提升心脏介入操作的速度、精度和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声有所进展，大多数经皮心脏介入仍依赖2D可视化，限制了深度感知和空间理解，亟需改进。

Method: 结合自定义机器视觉算法（用于5自由度导管跟踪）与3D打印机电编码器（测量导管滚动），实现完整的6自由度运动重建，并在沉浸式3D与传统2D视图下进行对比实验。

Result: 使用3D可视化的参与者平均用时54.6秒、导管移动1939毫米，优于2D组的267.5秒和7854毫米；3D模式速度提升5倍以上，导管移动减少约5倍，且定位更精准、变异性更低，导航更平滑。

Conclusion: XR-based 3D可视化系统能显著提高心脏介入培训的效率、精确性和运动控制能力，具有较高的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [165] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的潜在世界建模框架GWM-VLA，用于提升视觉-语言-动作模型在多视角下的鲁棒性和操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常独立编码多视角图像并忽略几何关系，导致在视觉和环境变化下性能下降。

Method: 采用几何感知的多视角状态编码、全局上下文条件的目标视角预测以及由机器人动作监督的共享潜在动作表示。使用VGGT-Ω聚合多视角观测，并预测选定目标视角的下一步patch token。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中实验表明，GWM-VLA在面对视觉和环境变化时具有更强的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: GWM-VLA通过保留多视角几何信息和共享动作表示，显著提升了VLA模型在复杂场景中的泛化与控制能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [166] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有可重构脊柱结构的履带机器人TRASER，通过调节关节位置和质心移动，显著提升了在非结构化环境中的越障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带机器人在非结构化环境中应用广泛，但其越障能力受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的权衡。为突破这一限制，研究旨在提升机器人的地形适应能力。

Method: TRASER采用基于tape-spring的可变关节机制，实现关节点和内部质量的重新定位；通过几何与静力学模型分析关节点和质心位置对台阶、沟壑等障碍通过性能的影响，并进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，TRASER可跨越高达机器人体长74%的台阶、66%的悬空平台和59%的沟壑，越障能力为目前履带机器人中最高水平。

Conclusion: TRASER通过可调关节与质心控制机制有效解决了履带机器人在可达性与稳定性之间的矛盾，显著提升了复杂地形下的通行能力。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [167] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于凸多面体的可微符号距离函数（Hölder signed distance），通过引入可微的Hölder最小和最大算子，实现了闭式计算，具有高效性、可微性和保持符号信息的优点，并验证了其在机器人控制中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的欧几里得符号距离函数（SDF）并非处处可微，而现有替代方法常牺牲可微性、符号信息或计算效率，限制了其在实时优化场景（如机器人运动规划与控制）中的应用。

Method: 提出了可微的Hölder最小与最大算子，并将其替换传统SDF公式中的min-max操作，从而构建出闭式可计算的Hölder符号距离函数，支持GPU并行化。

Result: 相比现有方法，该方法无需迭代、无收敛问题，在运行时性能上表现更优，并在机械臂控制实验中验证了其实际适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Hölder符号距离函数在保持符号信息的同时实现了完全可微和高效计算，适用于需要实时优化的机器人控制任务。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [168] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高密度拼图式存储系统的新型多机器人路径规划方法，通过利用无需重定位的存储特性，在保证无死锁和完整性的前提下实现高效的存取操作，并展示了接近线性的性能扩展和对不确定出库序列的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化仓库中，提高存储密度通常会牺牲检索效率。拼图式存储虽能提升容量，但多机器人协调困难且易发生死锁。本文旨在解决高密度存储下的高效、可扩展且无死锁的存取问题。

Method: 提出一种在线、优先级驱动的多智能体路径规划算法，利用无需重定位的排列特性，在矩形网格且单边出入的结构中进行存储与检索规划，结合几何可行性与执行效率。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在机器人数量增加时实现接近线性的完工时间改善，最高可扩展至机器人数量等于网格宽度；同时算法具有零额外开销的鲁棒性，能应对出库顺序不确定性而几乎不影响执行速度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合特定存储不变性与路径规划，实现了高密度存储环境下的可扩展、无死锁且高效的多机器人协同，为最大容量下的有序存取问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [169] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于回旋曲线并结合自适应前瞻机制的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略，显著提升了路径跟踪精度与行驶稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高自动驾驶车辆在复杂道路条件下的横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度，减少乘客不适与侧翻风险。

Method: 采用基于欧拉螺旋（回旋线）的平滑曲率过渡方法，结合实时车辆动力学与道路几何特征自适应调整前瞻距离，并设计准反馈控制算法与先导滤波器以生成最优转向输入并补偿系统延迟。

Result: 通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真验证，所提控制器在多种驾驶场景下均表现出优异的横向控制性能，有效降低偏离路径误差并提升响应速度与稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法在提升自动驾驶车辆横向控制性能方面具有显著优势，未来可拓展至高速场景及铰接车辆的优化控制。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [170] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoCoNav框架，结合在线共形校准与运行时验证规划，实现对预测误差的自适应调节，并在保证可行性的同时提升机器人在人群中的安全高效导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理行人运动预测不确定性时存在振荡行为、过度保守或模型受限等问题，难以兼顾安全性与导航效率。

Method: 采用针对时间范围的共形比例-积分控制器动态调整轨迹误差边界，以保障长期经验覆盖率；通过“先松弛后验证”规划器，在软约束MPC生成标称轨迹后，结合应急动作一起基于校准后的误差界进行可行性认证。

Result: 仿真和四足机器人实验表明，CoCoNav在避障能力、任务成功率和导航效率方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CoCoNav能有效应对动态环境中变化的预测误差，在保持求解可行性的同时实现安全高效的群体导航。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [171] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无人机-无人车协同导航框架，用于高海拔积雪地形，通过改进的U-Net实现实时道路分割，并结合EKF定位与视觉跟踪实现稳定导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高海拔积雪地形中，传统导航方法因能见度低和地面不稳定而失效，需要更鲁棒的协同导航方案。

Method: 设计了一种轻量级U-Net架构并采用新型合成积雪数据增强技术进行道路分割；使用EKF融合GPS与IMU数据实现UAV定位；通过YOLOv5和RGB-D相机深度信息实现UGV视觉跟踪；并采用动态路径规划应对积雪遮挡。

Result: 道路分割准确率达到96.5%；UAV定位最大误差为±0.5米；系统能在严重遮挡环境下实现低偏差导航。

Conclusion: 所提出的协同导航框架在高海拔积雪环境中表现出高精度与强鲁棒性，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [172] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种通过在机器人指尖内部设计不同硬度材料（中心为Shore E10，侧面为Shore A60）来提升对薄平物体（如硬币）抓取成功率的方法，无需传统指甲结构即可形成几何约束，扩展了有效抓取的操作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人抓取薄平物体（如硬币）时依赖重新定向、接触底面或添加刚性指甲机制，限制了灵活性和实用性。研究旨在探索不依赖物理指甲、仅通过材料布局优化即可增强抓取性能的新方法。

Method: 设计并制造具有混合刚度的仿人指尖：中心柔软（Shore E10），侧边较硬（Shore A60），并与全软（E10）指尖对比。使用自动化装置执行斜向旋转指尖捏取动作，测试在多种水平接近距离、垂直位移和手指旋转条件下的抓取表现。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在多数测试条件下抓取成功率显著高于均匀软指尖，且在无指甲情况下仍能有效形成几何约束并稳定抓取；而无指甲设计在所有36种条件下均失败。但在硬币沿手指轴位置变化时，均匀软指尖表现更优，显示出条件依赖性权衡。优化后，两种指尖均可抓取六种日本硬币。

Conclusion: 薄物体抓取的有效范围不仅取决于垫层柔软度，还与刚度在指尖内的分布位置有关。内部边界的设计可作为提升抓取性能的关键变量，为无指甲机器人指尖设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [173] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的多模态概率融合（MMPF）框架，用于检测和纠正机器人演示数据中的指令-轨迹不匹配（ITM）问题，显著提升了语言-行为映射的准确性，并通过实验验证了其在策略学习中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉-语言-动作策略训练中使用的演示数据可能存在行为正确但语言指令错误的隐患，即指令-轨迹不匹配（ITM），这类错误难以察觉但会破坏模型对语言与行为关系的学习，因此需要有效的后验审计方法。

Method: 提出多模态概率融合（MMPF）框架，将每种模态视为专家，通过局部邻域一致性与全局原型相似性估计任务标签分布，并采用预测熵加权的方式在专家乘积模型中融合多模态信息，实现无需训练的ITM检测与标签校正。

Result: 在注入ITM的LIBERO基准和含噪声的真实机器人数据上，MMPF在ITM检测和标签修正准确率方面均表现最佳；下游策略学习实验表明，审计可提升需语言消歧任务的性能；真实机器人实验验证了过滤或重标记演示数据的不同权衡。

Conclusion: MMPF是一种有效的训练-free审计方法，能可靠识别并纠正指令-轨迹不匹配，改善语言引导的机器人策略学习效果，为构建更鲁棒的多模态策略提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [174] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过引入3D轨迹场的时空知识来增强世界动作模型（WAMs）在视频预测与动作生成中的表现，提升空间理解、执行精度和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs主要在2D像素空间建模，导致与3D动作执行空间存在表示鸿沟；当前3D方法未能充分捕捉3D结构的动态演化，限制了模型对时空轨迹的理解。

Method: 提出4D-WAM，通过表示对齐将3D轨迹场的时空知识注入WAMs；设计两种互补目标：运动对齐（对齐相邻帧间时序特征变化）和目的地对齐（最小化源帧与目标帧间的注意力相似性分布差距），实现局部运动监督与长程目标引导。

Result: 在多种基础模型上进行的分布内与分布外实验表明，该方法显著提升了模型的空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化性和通用性。

Conclusion: 4D-WAM通过引入局部与全局的4D感知机制，有效弥合了2D视觉表征与3D动作空间之间的差距，为WAMs学习轨迹级时空表示提供了通用且有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [175] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方向感知的下一最佳视角（DA-NBV）策略，用于提升海上船舶的3D重建质量，通过引入方向观测统计和位置优势场（PAF）显著提高了重建完整性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NBV方法主要建模空间占用，忽略方向观测历史，导致船舶复杂结构和自遮挡情况下重建不完整；同时海况动态变化和资源限制对扫描效率提出更高要求。

Method: 提出DA-NBV策略，增强传统占用状态表示，引入可学习的位置优势场（PAF）利用方向信息指导视点选择，采用局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖塑形奖励以提高扫描效率，并构建面向船舶的SeaShip-3D数据集与可配置海况仿真环境。

Result: 在不同升沉、横摇和纵摇条件下实验表明，DA-NBV将重建完整性提高约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，并实现更高的路径效率。

Conclusion: DA-NBV通过融合方向感知机制有效提升了动态海况下船舶3D重建的质量与效率，具备较强的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了任务状态范围（TSH）作为衡量智能体在长时程具身任务中跟踪任务相关世界状态能力的新指标，并发布了基于RoboGraph编译的包含588个场景的基准测试，实验表明现有先进模型在高TSH任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要通过动作序列长度和子任务复杂性来评估难度，忽略了智能体需持续跟踪由探索和环境动态引起的世界状态变化这一关键挑战。

Method: 提出RoboGraph任务编译器，将时空因果依赖关系（包括执行中的意外失败与干预）转化为可执行的符号图，构建不同TSH的任务，并在此基础上建立包含84个场景、588个episode的基准测试。

Result: 在语义和视觉闭环环境中对15种先进智能体模型的实验显示，大多数模型在面对较高TSH时表现下降，暴露出其在长期维持、探索和更新任务相关状态方面的显著缺陷。

Conclusion: 任务状态范围（TSH）是评估长时程具身智能体性能的关键维度，当前模型在此方面存在明显不足，需进一步研究以提升状态跟踪能力。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>


### [177] [PhysX-CoT: Structured Physical Reasoning from a Single Image to Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08053)
*Jie Huang,Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Fangli Mou,Chen Qian,Yuqiang Fang,Junhao Fan,Kaixin Zhang,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysX-CoT 提出一种显式的结构化物理推理方法，用于从单张图像生成仿真就绪的3D资产，通过分解、2D/3D定位、关系、几何和表面线索等中间状态提升生成质量与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将图像到3D资产生成视为黑箱输出过程，缺乏对中间物理状态的建模与监督，导致部分放置与形状纠缠、物理一致性差。

Method: 将单图3D资产生成建模为有序的、机器可解析的物理推理链（CoT），显式建模部件级状态（如分解、2D/3D定位、关系、粗略几何、表面线索），并分别进行监督；几何因子化为3D框（位置）和局部码（形状）；采用CoT对齐的GRPO优化多个目标。

Result: 在统一重训所有基线模型的协议下，PhysX-CoT在几何、尺度和物理属性指标上优于最强的端到端基线；消融实验验证了显式状态的有效性而非装饰性；在Unreal Engine 5中生成资产具有高有效性的解析、碰撞和运动能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模物理推理链能显著提升单图3D资产生成的质量与物理合理性，为仿真就绪内容创建提供了更可控、可解释且高性能的新范式。

Abstract: Simulation-ready 3D assets are central to robotics and embodied AI. Generating them from a single image is usually framed as a vision-language model that emits a serialized asset for a decoder to turn into geometry and physical fields, leaving the image-to-3D reasoning implicit. We argue the limiting factor is this output-centric view: part placement and local shape are entangled in one global-coordinate token stream, and the intermediate physical states are never exposed for supervision, conditioning, or verification. PhysX-CoT instead casts single-image asset generation as an explicit structured physical reasoning process, an ordered and machine-parseable trajectory of part-level states covering decomposition, 2D and 3D grounding, relations, coarse geometry, and surface cues that we separately supervise, use to condition geometry, and treat as reward targets. Geometry is factorized so that 3D boxes carry placement and local codes carry shape, and CoT-aligned GRPO optimizes parse validity, grounding, geometry, placement, and physical consistency. Under a unified protocol that retrains all learned baselines on the same backbone, data, and frozen decoder, PhysX-CoT outperforms the closest full-task baseline across geometry, scale, and physical-attribute metrics. Oracle, token-matched, and state-order controls show the explicit states are functional rather than cosmetic, and in Unreal Engine~5 the generated assets parse, collide, and articulate at high validity.

</details>


### [178] [SurgWMBench: A Vision-Based Benchmark for World-Modeling Surgical Instrument Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08070)
*Huanrong Liu,Weiliang Huang,Bob Zhang,Weichao Cai,Chunlin Tian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SurgWMBench，一个面向短期手术运动规划与动态预测的视觉基准，用于评估手术世界模型在器械运动预测和连续 rollout 下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法多集中于阶段识别或工具轨迹预测，缺乏对手术状态与器械运动联合建模的能力；同时缺少针对运动规划性能的公开数据集和标准化评估协议。

Method: 提出SurgWMBench，基于术中图像序列和历史器械轨迹，构建一个以视觉为中心的世界模型评估基准，重点评估近期器械运动预测精度及在连续预测和输入扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 该基准支持对几何准确性、时间一致性和动作可行性进行评估，弥补了以往仅依赖FVD等生成质量指标的不足。

Conclusion: SurgWMBench为手术世界模型提供了更贴近实际规划需求的评估框架，推动模型从‘看得清’向‘可预测、可行动’发展。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models that move beyond recognizing the current surgical step or imitating expert demonstrations, and instead anticipate how instrument motion reshapes subsequent operative states. Most surgical video understanding methods focus on recognizing phases, actions, or workflow states, while providing limited support for explicitly modeling instrument motion. Conversely, existing tool motion prediction methods can forecast instrument trajectories, but they generally do not capture the coupled evolution of future surgical video states. World models offer a natural framework for jointly modeling visual state transitions and instrument motion dynamics. However, existing surgical world model studies remain largely centered on visual generation quality, relying on generation-oriented metrics such as FVD and CD-FVD. These metrics are poorly aligned with instrument motion planning, as they do not directly measure whether predicted trajectories are geometrically accurate, temporally coherent, or actionable for downstream planning. This limitation is partly structural, since the field lacks public datasets and standardized evaluation protocols that provide the benchmarking infrastructure needed to assess motion-centric capabilities in surgical world models. In this paper, we introduce SurgWMBench, a vision-based benchmark for short-horizon surgical motion planning and dynamics prediction. Given intraoperative image sequences and historical instrument trajectory, SurgWMBench evaluates both near-future instrument motion prediction and stability under continuous rollout or input perturbations.

</details>


### [179] [Event-Time Hybrid Optimal Control for Robotic Table Tennis Serves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08157)
*Thomas Gossard,Till Köpff,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件时间最优控制的方法，用于生成满足动力学约束且具有高旋转变量的机器人乒乓球发球动作，相比传统方法显著提升了求解效率，并在真实机器人上实现了准确的落点与旋转控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在尊重机器人运动学和动力学限制的同时，实现高速度、高旋转且符合比赛规则（如必须触台并过网）的乒乓球发球，需要一种能够处理非线性飞行与碰撞动力学的新型控制方法。

Method: 将旋转变量控制的发球生成问题建模为一个事件时间最优控制问题（OCP），优化球拍撞击时的速度、姿态以及球的弹跳、过网和落地时刻，并在相位边界直接施加触台和过网约束；随后通过第二个OCP将球拍状态转化为完整的、满足关节限幅的动力学可行轨迹。

Result: 数值仿真和KUKA Agilus机器人实验表明，所提方法相较固定步长法将中位求解时间减少了4.1倍，保持了相当的落点精度、旋转变量精度和发球有效性；实验证明可实现上旋、下旋和侧旋发球，平均落点误差为13.1±7.3 cm，最高转速达30 rps。

Conclusion: 事件时间最优控制能高效生成物理合理、动力学可行且满足比赛规则的乒乓球发球动作，有效处理空气动力学与碰撞的非线性效应。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis serves require high ball velocity and spin while respecting the robot's kinodynamic limits. Unlike rally strokes, a valid serve must also bounce on the server's side and clear the net, yielding a hybrid system with nonlinear flight and impact dynamics. We formulate spin-controlled serve generation as an event-time \ac{OCP} that optimizes the racket impact velocity and orientation together with the bounce, net-crossing, and landing times, enabling direct enforcement at phase boundaries of table-bounce and net-clearance constraints. The racket velocity and orientation are then converted into a complete kinodynamically feasible motion through a second \ac{OCP} enforcing joint-position, velocity, and torque limits. We evaluate the method numerically and on a KUKA Agilus robot. Compared with a fixed-step formulation with root localization, the proposed event-time formulation reduces the median solve time by a factor of 4.1 while maintaining comparable landing accuracy, spin accuracy, and serve validity. Real-robot experiments demonstrate controlled placement and topspin, backspin, and sidespin serves, with a mean landing error of $13.1 \pm 7.3$~cm and spin rates up to 30~rps. These results show that event-time optimal control efficiently generates physically valid, kinodynamically feasible serves while accounting for nonlinear aerodynamic and impact effects.

</details>


### [180] [Multi-modal Interactive Control of Robotic Arm based on Offline Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08183)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并实现了基于本地大语言模型的“苏格拉底模型-ChatGLM”，用于在PyBullet平台上进行机器人臂的多模态交互控制，具有处理复杂多步长视野操作任务的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大多数大型语言模型（如ChatGPT）不开源且使用成本高，因此需要一种低成本、稳定且可自由使用的开源本地部署方案来开发创意具身AI算法。

Method: 基于离线大语言模型，结合多模态输入，在PyBullet仿真环境中实现名为“苏格拉底模型-ChatGLM”的算法，用于机器人臂的交互式控制。

Result: 该方法能够在文本和图像融合的复杂多步长视野任务中展现出卓越的潜力，并实现高效的多模态控制。

Conclusion: “苏格拉底模型-ChatGLM”是一种高效、低成本的本地化解决方案，适用于复杂多模态机器人操控任务，推动了开源具身AI的发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly revolutionized the modern society with numerous advanced interactions between humans and AI agents, whereas the usage of most large language models including ChatGPT are not friendly open-sourced and must require the users paying a lot for such AI services continuously. Therefore, deploying open-sourced large language models on local servers can be considered as an efficient approach to design and implement creative embodied AI algorithms with lower cost and more stable free usage. Inspired by this ordinary motivation, we originally propose and implement the "Socratic Models-ChatGLM", which is a well-performed algorithm for multi-modal interactive control of robotic arm based on offline large language models via the facile PyBullet platform, even presents extraordinary potential to address complicated text-image integrated multi-step long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [181] [Spatiotemporal Context-dependent Personalized Movement Compensation in Delayed Telemanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08200)
*Sai Jiang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种个性化的运动缩放方法，用于改善延迟遥操作中的任务精度，实验表明该方法在多种条件下均能显著提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信延迟影响遥操作机器人的视觉-运动协调，降低任务精度，传统统一缩放增益无法适应个体和情境差异。

Method: 为每位参与者拟合个性化的、针对延迟、方向和距离的运动缩放参数，并通过虚拟仿真和遥外手术机器人进行实验验证。

Result: 运动缩放在多个指标上显著提升了操作性能，关键指标提升达20-25%，尤其在长延迟下效果更明显；个性化参数在短距离向内运动中进一步提高了准确性。

Conclusion: 个性化运动缩放有助于提升遥操作的安全性和精确性，可作为融合上下文信息的自适应框架的基础。

Abstract: Communication delay remains a central challenge in telerobotics, where it disrupts visuomotor coordination and reduces task precision. Motion scaling is an effective countermeasure to delay-induced overshoot, yet typical deployments rely on uniform gains that neglect individual and contextual variability. We propose a human-centered method that fits personalized delay-, direction-, and distance-specific scaling parameters for each participant. We conducted experiments with twenty participants who performed delayed reaching tasks in a virtual simulator. Scaling gains were computed to minimize mean overshoot in simulation in each combination of experimental conditions. Evaluation was done in simulation and on a telesurgical robot to evaluate assistance benefits. Performance was assessed across multiple delays, distances, and movement directions using overshoot, endpoint error, trajectory smoothness, economy of motion, and a composite error-time metric. Motion scaling consistently improved performance relative to unassisted trials, yielding up to 20-25% performance gains in key metrics. Effects were most pronounced at longer delays. Personalization demonstrated additional accuracy benefits for inward reaching at a short distance under moderate delay. The results highlight the potential of personalized scaling as a foundation for more adaptive frameworks that integrate contextual information to improve the safety and precision of teleoperated procedures.

</details>


### [182] [Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08273)
*Hwanhee Kim,Jaehyun Jang,Seungmin Cha,Hyeonseo Yun,Donghoon Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ALVA方法，利用预训练的视觉-语言模型对基于动作和语言条件的视频进行评估，以生成轨迹级进度分数，在模拟3D家庭环境中表现出低误报率，并在闭环策略优化中提供比静态图像和嵌入基线更有效的反馈。


<details>
